declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t1136 = call ptr @"example.com/stdtest/io.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t1138 = alloca i32
  %t1137 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t1136, ptr %t1138, ptr null)
  %t1139 = icmp ne i32 %t1137, 0
  br i1 %t1139, label %main.async.fail.106, label %main.async.ok.106
main.async.fail.106:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.106:
  %t1140 = load i32, ptr %t1138
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t1140
}
